**Admin lists keep their page after save/delete (2026-11-11, owner report: editing a product from page 3 of Admin › Products and saving returned to page 1; asked to fix the same issue on every paginated CMS table; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):** *Analysis:* 17 admin tables are server-paginated (products, orders, trashed orders, incomplete orders, customers, coupons, flash sales, bundles, brands, sub-categories, blogs, reviews, returns, received SMS, newsletter, contact messages, failed jobs, audit trail; 20 rows/page, audit trail 30). Actions that `back()` to the list (reviews, returns, received SMS, contact messages, failed jobs, incomplete orders, newsletter delete, order trash restore) already kept the page; every `update`/`destroy`/bulk action that did `redirect()->route('admin.<x>.index')` (products, blogs, brands, coupons, flash sales, bundles, sub-categories, customer anonymize, order trash, order permanent delete) dropped page and filters, and so did every form/detail page's Back/Cancel link. *Change:* new `RememberAdminListUrl` middleware (alias `admin.remember-list`, on the main admin route group) stores the query string of each successful, non-prefetch GET to an `admin.*.index` route (plus `admin.orders.trashed`) in the session, and forgets it when the plain list is opened; `App\Support\AdminListUrl::redirect()` rebuilds the URL from the route's own path plus that query string (never an arbitrary URL) and replaces the bare redirects above; `store` actions deliberately still open page 1, where the new record appears. Shared prop `admin_list_urls` (admins only) + `useAdminListUrl()` hook feed the Back/Cancel links on the create/edit pages of products, blogs, brands, coupons, flash sales, bundles and sub-categories, and on customer/order detail, trashed orders, incomplete-order convert and newsletter compose. `AdminListUrl::redirectIfPastLastPage()` in all 18 paginated list actions sends a page that no longer exists (last row on the last page just deleted) to the last real page, keeping the filters, instead of showing an empty table. Tests: new `Admin/AdminListPositionTest` (10: update and delete return to page+filters, plain list forgets, prefetch doesn't overwrite, lists don't share positions, create still opens page 1, edit page gets the remembered URL, past-last-page redirect with/without filters, empty page 1 not redirected); `tests/Feature/Admin` 1042 tests green; full suite 2972: 2897 passed, 3 failed (the local-`.env` `ProductionTransportSecurityTest` trio), 72 skipped; Pint, ESLint, Prettier and `tsc` clean on changed files. Not yet checked in a real browser. No migration.

**CI flake fix (2026-11-11, after the push of `3c052c5..65bf8f9`; test-only, COMMITTED, NOT PUSHED):** the deploy `checks` job failed one test, `FlashSaleCostWarningTest` 'an admin who cannot edit products never receives cost prices from the product search' (BIZ-039, not touched by this session's work): it asserts the whole JSON response does not contain the text `600`/`650` (the product's and variant's cost), but the response also carries factory-random fields (`sku` like `aq-00928`, variant SKU `VAR-08640`, stock up to 500) that occasionally contain those digits (roughly 2% of runs; 0 of 40 local runs, 1 CI run). Fixed by pinning `sku` and `stock_quantity` on the product and variant in that test so only a real cost leak can match; the sentinel check itself is unchanged. 60 consecutive local runs of the file: 0 failures. No application code changed.

**CI regression fix (2026-11-11, after the push of `9a73d8b..906a5e4`, deploy `checks` failed: 4 tests):** (1) *Mine, a real regression:* the #23 fix (`8f8cc19`) added `prepareForValidation()` to `PlaceOrderRequest`, which silently overrides the `NormalizesBangladeshiPhone` trait's method of the same name, so checkout stopped normalising Bangla digits / `+880` numbers (3 `BangladeshiPhoneTest` cases; FE-012 behaviour broken on checkout). Fixed by aliasing the trait method (`prepareForValidation as protected normalizePhoneInput`) and calling it first; no other request class overrides it (checked all six users of the trait). I had wrongly written those failures off as environmental when I saw them locally; they were the first sign of this. (2) *My new test:* `NewsletterChunkRetryTest` passed alone but failed in the full suite because `queue:work`'s default `--memory=128` made each in-process worker call stop after one job once the single PHPUnit process had grown past 128 MB; reproduced locally with 200 MB of ballast, fixed with `--memory=4096` in the test helper. Full suite locally: 2851 tests, 2776 passed, 3 failed — the `ProductionTransportSecurityTest` trio, which depends on the local `.env` `APP_URL` (passes with `APP_URL=http://localhost`, and was not among CI's failures). Not yet pushed; the previous push's deploy was blocked by the failing checks job, so nothing from it reached production.

**Product page: no fake brand label or fake rating (2026-11-11, tester report; FIXED_LOCALLY, COMMITTED, NOT PUSHED):** in the product page's buy sidebar, a product with no brand showed the text 'GENUINE PRODUCT' as a link to `route('brand.show', '#')`, a 404; next to it the rating fell back to a made-up '5.0' (`average_rating?.toFixed(1) || '5.0'`) when the product had no approved reviews. Fix, frontend only (`resources/js/pages/catalog/ProductDetail.tsx`): the brand link renders only when `product.brand` is set (no label otherwise); it needs no `is_active` check because `Product::isPubliclyVisible()` already 404s a product whose brand is inactive. The star rating renders only when `reviews_count > 0` and `average_rating` is set, now followed by the count, e.g. '4.3 (12)'; otherwise 'No reviews yet'. No server change: `ProductController::show()` already sends `average_rating: null` and `reviews_count: 0` for an unreviewed product (FSA-18 test). Other rating surfaces (cards, listings) were checked by grep and have no such fallback. Not changed: the generic '100% Genuine' trust badge under the buy buttons and 'Genuine Products' on the cart page are store-wide claims, not per-product labels. *Tests:* new `ProductDetailBrandLabelTest` (3: no brand → `product.brand` null; a branded product sends its brand and the brand page loads; an inactive-brand product 404s); `ProductRatingBreakdownTest`'s stale comment about the '5.0' fallback corrected. Both files 8/8; `--filter='Product|Review|Brand'` 653 tests, 639 passed, 14 skipped, 0 failed; Pint clean; ESLint 0 errors (3 pre-existing hook warnings in the same file); tsc clean for the file; `npm run build` OK. Not checked in a live browser.

**Cross-device cart sync without a page load (2026-11-12, owner request; owner chose option 1 of three: polling + focus check + a version check, over polling alone or Pusher; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):** before, a logged-in customer's cart reached another device only on that device's next page load, and every save replaced the whole server cart, so a device holding an older copy could delete lines another device (or the AI assistant, wishlist→cart, checkout clean-up) had added. *Server:* `CartManager::version()` = hash of the cart's rows (product, variant, bundle, bundle_unit_quantity, quantity) and coupon, `EMPTY_VERSION` for none — computed from the rows, so every existing write path changes it with no extra code; `serialize()` adds `version`. `syncUserCart()` gains optional `expectedVersion`, checked in `writeUserCart()` after the user and cart locks, before any write, throwing new `CartVersionConflictException`; `CartController::sync()` passes `base_version` (new optional `SyncCartRequest` rule, string ≤64) except for the login merge, and answers 409 `{conflict, cart}`. No `base_version` = the old unconditional save (pages still open across the deploy). New `GET /cart/state` (`cart.state`, same auth group as `cart.sync`, hardcoded `cart-state` limiter 60/min per user): `{changed:false, version}` when the sent version is current (cheap: no relations loaded, `versionForUser()`), else the serialized cart. *Client:* `cartStore` persists `serverVersion` + `serverBase` (quantity per item_key), set by `hydrateCart()`. `app.tsx` sends `base_version`; on 409 `rebaseOntoServerCart()` re-applies this tab's own changes (removed here → removed; added/changed here → this tab's quantity; everything else the server's) and saves again, up to 3 times, then shows the server cart with a toast. `pollCartState()` every 15 s while the tab is visible, plus on focus/visibilitychange; skipped while a save is unsaved or in flight, and on `checkout/`, `order/`, `landing/`, `admin/`, `errors/` pages; its answer is dropped if anything changed locally while it was out. A coupon applied on the Cart page (browser-only) is kept when a change arrives from elsewhere. Same-browser tabs still sync through the `storage` event. *Tests:* new `CartCrossDeviceSyncTest` (12: current version saves and returns a new version; stale version 409 and keeps the other device's line; a non-sync write also stales the version; EMPTY_VERSION can create but not overwrite another device's new cart; no base_version still unconditional; login merge ignores a stale version; >64 chars 422; poll unchanged/changed/no cart; poll never returns another customer's cart; guest 401). Cart/Checkout/Bundle/Wishlist/AiAssistant/MobilePayment/Coupon/Landing/Order sweep 1249 tests, 1228 passed, 21 skipped; full SQLite suite 3025 tests, 2953 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed. Pint clean, ESLint 0 errors, tsc clean, `npm run build` OK, `ziggy:generate` run. `CartConcurrencyRaceTest` (MySQL-only) grepped: its `syncUserCart()` calls still fit (new parameter optional, last). *Not verified:* the browser side (merge, polling) has no JS test runner here and was not run on two real devices; the MySQL race test was not re-run.

**Start-up read no page data under Inertia v3 (2026-11-12, found while investigating an owner report about cross-device sync; owner approved the fix; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, frontend only):** `resources/js/app.tsx`'s start-up readers (`readInitialUserId()`, `initialPageCarriesAuth()`, `readInitialCart()`, `readInitialBundleRecipes()`, `readInitialComponent()`) read `<div id="app" data-page>`, but Inertia v3 renders the page data into `<script data-page="app" type="application/json">` (vendor `inertia-laravel` `View/Components/App.php`), so they always got nothing. Effect: every full page load of a logged-in customer ran `applyLogout()` first (stored last user, no user read), removing the login marker and writing an empty cart and wishlist to localStorage, which every other open tab picked up as an empty cart until the `navigate` handler re-hydrated it a moment later; and FE-005's error-page guard never applied (a full-load 500/503 showed an empty cart and wishlist until the next normal page). FE-008's guest-cart merge happened to work only because the `navigate` handler did it. Correction recorded: unsaved local cart changes are not rescued by this fix — a reload still replaces them with the server cart, by design. *Fix:* one `readInitialPage()` (parsed once) reads the v3 script element, falling back to the v2 attribute; the five readers use it with unchanged defaults. Because start-up now really applies the first page's cart (or merges a just-logged-in guest cart), the first `navigate` event no longer re-applies that same page's older cart (`initialPageCartApplied`, consumed at the top of the handler) — found by the browser test below, where it overwrote the merge result. Order-confirmation clean-up and FE-011 unsaved-change handling in that event are unchanged. *Verified in headless Chrome (local dev, throwaway customer, permanently deleted after):* before the fix, a second tab saw `last_user REMOVED`, `cart REMOVED`, `cart items=0` during a reload; after it, no storage events and no `cart.sync` calls on reload; a guest's cart merges once on a full-page login and shows immediately (merge sent once, no doubled quantity after reload); logout still clears the cart and marker; cross-device sync still empties the desktop cart ~8 s after the phone removes. ESLint 0 errors, tsc clean, `npm run build` OK. No PHP changed. *Also recorded:* the previous session's first cleanup of that throwaway customer only soft-deleted it (User uses SoftDeletes); it was restored for this test and then force-deleted, with its cart.

**Cross-device and cross-tab wishlist sync (2026-11-12, owner request: 'the same thing for wishlist, it will behave like cart'; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):** before, a logged-in customer's wishlist reached another device or even another tab of the same browser only on that tab's next page load, and a heart click was a blind toggle, so a device showing an older wishlist flipped a product the wrong way (tapping 'remove' on a product already removed elsewhere added it back). *Server:* `WishlistController::toggle()` accepts optional `wishlisted` (boolean, the state chosen on screen): idempotent set — false deletes if present, true creates if absent (a unique-index race is caught, `wishlists` has unique(user_id, product_id)); without it, the old toggle (pages open across the deploy). `CartController::state()` (the 15 s `cart.state` poll) also returns the account's `wishlist_ids` on both branches. *Client:* `wishlistStore.toggleWishlist()` sends `wishlisted: !isSelected` and counts clicks (`changeSeq`) and unanswered saves (`writesInFlight`, exported via `wishlistLocalChangeState()`). `app.tsx`'s poller (renamed `pollAccountSyncState()`) applies the ids unless a click happened since the poll left or is still saving; the cart part keeps its own rules (now evaluated after the response rather than skipping the whole poll while the cart is busy). A `storage` listener rehydrates the wishlist from other same-browser tabs. On `account/Wishlist` (product cards from its own `wishlist` prop) a change from elsewhere triggers `router.reload({ only: ['wishlist', 'wishlist_ids', 'cart'] })`, with cart and ids fresh so the following navigate handler doesn't re-apply older data. *Tests:* new `WishlistCrossDeviceSyncTest` (6: stale remove stays removed; stale add keeps one row; explicit add/remove persist; no state still toggles; non-boolean 422; poll returns only the customer's own ids). `CartCrossDeviceSyncTest`'s two exact-JSON poll assertions updated for the added `wishlist_ids` key. Wishlist/Cart/Account/HandleInertia/Checkout sweep 487 tests, 479 passed, 8 skipped, 0 failed; Pint clean, ESLint 0 errors, tsc clean, `npm run build` OK. *Verified in headless Chrome (local dev, throwaway customer, force-deleted after):* phone adds → desktop's open Wishlist page reloads its list on the next poll and the desktop's other tab gets the id; another same-browser tab adds → the Wishlist page updates at once; desktop removes, then the phone (still showing 'Wishlisted') removes → sends `wishlisted:false`, product stays removed. *Seen, pre-existing, not changed:* a full load of an SSR product page for a wishlisted product logs a React hydration mismatch (the server renders 'Wishlist' since it can't read the browser's saved wishlist; the client shows 'Wishlisted' and React re-renders that tree).

**Wishlist hearts hydration mismatch (2026-11-12, found during the wishlist-sync browser test; owner asked for a meticulous fix; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, frontend only):** *Root cause:* zustand v5's `useStore` gives React a server snapshot built from the store's initial (empty) state, so a selector that reads its `state` argument renders the same as the server during hydration. `ProductCard` and `ProductDetail` selected `state.isWishlisted(id)`, a store method that reads live state via `get()`, so the browser's first render used the saved wishlist while the server (which can't see it) rendered 'not wishlisted'. *Effect, measured:* a headless-Chrome sweep of all 21 public storefront pages as a guest (full loads) was clean with empty storage; with a saved cart and wishlist, 7 failed: brand, category, flash-sales, products, search (ProductCard heart: 'attributes didn't match' — React keeps the server's attributes, so a wishlisted product's heart stayed visibly un-filled with the wrong aria-label, a real display bug) and both product pages (Wishlist button text: React discarded the server HTML and re-rendered the page). Header counts were already safe (they read `state`). *Fix:* both selectors read `state.productIds.includes(id)`; the store's `isWishlisted()` (no other callers) removed so the trap can't return; doc comments on the wishlist state and on cart getters (which read `get()`; fine in handlers and on the no-SSR cart/checkout pages). The unused starter-kit `app-header.tsx` had the same pattern (`getItemCount()` in a selector) and was fixed the same way. *Verified:* the same sweep after the fix — 21/21 clean with empty storage and 21/21 with the saved cart + wishlist; after a full load the product page shows 'Wishlisted', listing hearts are filled with 'Remove … from wishlist', the header badge shows 2. Prettier, ESLint 0 errors (3 pre-existing hook warnings in ProductDetail), tsc clean, `npm run build` OK. No PHP changed. Guest-only test, no database records created.

**Product images per colour, and landing page without 'Area / Landmark' (2026-11-12, tester report; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; includes a migration):** (1) *Images:* on a colour+size product the admin could only tie an image to one colour/size pair (Red/L, Red/XL, …), so the same photo had to be uploaded once per size; a `color:` tag only resolved on colour-only products (it silently saved as untagged otherwise). *Fix:* migration `2026_11_11_210444_add_color_value_id_to_product_media_table` adds nullable `product_media.color_value_id` (FK attribute_values, nullOnDelete) and backfills it from each tagged image's variant colour, leaving `variant_id` untouched (non-destructive; `down()` drops the column). Admin: tag options list each colour once whenever the product has colours (`buildMediaTagOptions`), sizes only on size-only products; the edit form shows saved images (and older pair-tagged ones) as their colour. `Admin\ProductController::resolveMediaTag()` stores `color_value_id` for `color:` (plus the colour-only variant's id, as before), `variant_id` for `size:`, treats a legacy `pair:` as its colour, and ignores a colour/size the product has no variant of. `ProductVariant::media()` now returns the whole product's media (kept under that name so all 24 existing `variants.media` eager loads stay a single query; nothing else reads it — grepped front and back) and is `$hidden` from JSON; `ProductVariant::image` = own image first, else the colour's (images only, not videos). Every variant-image consumer (cart, checkout, order items, landing variant picker, bundles, incomplete orders, admin orders) goes through that accessor, unchanged. Storefront `ProductDetail`: selecting a variant jumps to its own image else its colour's; clicking a colour image selects that colour, keeping the current size when that combination exists. (2) *Landing page:* removed the optional 'Area / Landmark' input (it bound `address_line2`); the form still sends `address_line2: ''`, exactly like the regular checkout, which has no such input. Server validation (nullable) and the confirmation/order display of an existing landmark are unchanged. *Tests:* 4 new in `Admin/ProductVariantManagementTest` (colour image serves every size, Blue gets none; legacy pair tag counts as colour; unknown colour ignored; colour-only tag records colour and variant), new `ProductColorImageTest` (own image beats colour image; video never a thumbnail; product page sends each variant its image without per-variant `media`; a Red/XL cart line shows the Red image; listing `product_media` query count doesn't grow with products — mutation-checked: removing the listing's `variants.media` eager load fails it). Existing tag tests unchanged and green. Full SQLite suite 3040 tests, 2968 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed. Pint clean, ESLint 0 errors (2 pre-existing hook warnings in LandingPage), tsc clean, `npm run build` OK. *MySQL:* migration run on disposable `shoppydrive_test_colormedia` (identity verified; dropped after): migrated, rolled back the new migration, inserted a pair-tagged, a size-only and an untagged image, migrated again → pair image got Red with `variant_id` kept, the others unchanged; a new Red/XL variant's `image` returned the old Red/L photo; rollback kept all rows. *Not done:* the dev `ShoppyDrive` database is not migrated (needs owner approval); until it is, saving a product on the local site fails on the missing column.

**Admin field length and number limits (2026-11-12, owner request, not an audit finding; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, no migration):** *What:* every admin text field now has a context-sized limit (card/table/meta-tag length, not the 255 column size), enforced twice. *Backend:* `max:` rules added or tightened in `ProductRequest`, `Category/SubCategory/Brand/Coupon/FlashSale/ProductBundle/Blog/HomepageSection/CreateOrder/UpdateOrderShippingAddress/OrderPricingPreviewRequest`, and in the `Setting`, `Admin`, `Newsletter`, `PopularKeyword`, `BlockedIp`, `SmsPattern`, `HeroSlide`, `PromotionalBanner`, `Order`, `Return`, `OrderDeliveryVerification`, `Marketing`, `Security` and `LandingPage` controllers. Previously unbounded: product/brand/category/sub-category/bundle/homepage descriptions, product description (a `text` column) and blog content, newsletter body, `homepage metadata.free_delivery_amount` (stored with no rule at all). Number caps: money 99,999,999.99 (the `decimal(10,2)` columns), stock 9,999,999, sort orders 9,999, coupon uses 9,999,999 / per-user 1,000, free-delivery minimum 9,999, bundle and order line quantities 1,000, loyalty/referral settings. Password fields `max:255`. *Frontend:* `maxLength` on every admin text input/textarea (about 270 fields), `max` on number inputs, client `validate()` numbers aligned, opt-in `showCount` on shared `Input`/`Textarea` plus `CharCount` for raw fields, `RichTextEditor maxLength` (undoes input past the limit, shows HTML length), and `truncate`/`break-words` (with a hover title on truncated names) on admin list rows. *Existing data:* checked the local dev database against every new limit; two rows exceeded a proposed limit (About-page stat label 90, Contact-page hero description 446), so those two limits stay at 100 and 500. **Production was not checked**: a record already longer than a new limit will fail validation on its next save until shortened; run a read-only length check (`max(char_length(col))` per column) on production before deploying. Landing-page builder sections (`sections.*`) are now validated field by field. *Tests:* new `Admin/AdminFieldLimitsTest` (24, at-limit accepted and over-limit refused). Local run with the missing `mews/purifier` stubbed: 3,083 tests, failures only in HTML-sanitisation tests (stub), WebPush/`assertQueuedTimes` (outdated local `vendor`), `ScheduledDatesTimezoneTest` coupon (verified: fails identically with the original `CouponRequest`) and `ProductionTransportSecurityTest` (3 tests; this work touches nothing they cover, but I did not re-run them against the original code). *Follow-up (2026-11-12, About page):* hero description limit lowered to 200 (backend and form) and shown in full on `static/About` via a new opt-in `PageHero fullDescription` prop (other pages still clamp); the CMS eyebrow, stat value and stat label fields now show their counters; About stats are cards whose figures step down in size and wrap, and the Mission body/features no longer overflow (the cause was an unbroken-word `<h1>` in the saved mission body: columns now wrap with `overflow-wrap:anywhere`, headings inside the body are capped in size). Mission body is now limited to 400 *visible* characters (markup not counted; server rule plus `RichTextEditor maxTextLength`, which undoes input past it; the 20,000 HTML guard stays); the dev database's existing mission body (1,788 characters) must be shortened on its next save. `AdminFieldLimitsTest` is now 26. *Counters everywhere (2026-11-12):* every limited admin text field (about 220 more; passwords, search boxes, read-only key displays and hex-colour boxes excluded) now shows its `n/max` counter. A counter below a field shifts bottom-aligned neighbours, so rows were checked with a TypeScript-AST script (grid/flex rows mixing counters, `items-end`, counters inside flex rows): the About page stat/feature/value/team rows moved from `items-end` to `items-start`, counters that landed inside a flex/grid row (landing slug, hero feature boxes, delivery areas) were wrapped or moved outside, and the inline keyword editor draws its counter inside the input. Follow-up: about 50 fields had a doubled counter (two counter passes overlapped; the second missed the first because Prettier had indented it), which made fields taller and misaligned the About Mission row; the duplicates were removed and an AST check now confirms exactly one counter per limited field (242 with, 4 intentionally without: two hex-colour boxes and two slug fields whose counter sits outside the prefixed box). Not verified in a browser.  *Storefront field limits (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):* customer-facing inputs had almost no `maxLength` and the backend allowed 255 almost everywhere. Limits now sized by context, in both the inputs and the rules: name 100, email 150, phone 20 raw (the 11-digit Bangladeshi regex is unchanged), address line 200, city/state/country 60, postal code 10, address label 30, contact subject 150 and message 2,000 (was 5,000), review title 100 (body 2,000), return reason 1,000 (per-item 300), coupon code 50, order number 40, referral code 20, transaction id 40, passwords 128, search terms 64 (already). Rules changed in `ProfileValidationRules`, `PasswordValidationRules`, `CreateNewUser`, `PlaceOrderRequest`, `ProfileUpdateRequest`, `Convert/TrackIncompleteOrderRequest`, `Address/Contact/Review/ReturnRequest/Newsletter/LandingPage/EmailVerificationCode` controllers, the `/coupon/validate` route and `OrderTrackingController` (over-long lookup values count as not given). Counters only on long free text (contact message, review body, return reason, landing-page notes), each below its field in a single column so no side-by-side row changes height. New `StorefrontFieldLimitsTest`. Existing saved records over a new limit fail on their next save; the login password and price-filter number boxes have no new server cap. Production length check and a browser check still pending.  *Site identity / one logo everywhere (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, no migration):* the logo uploaded in Admin › Settings is now the complete lockup (mark + name + motto) and is drawn by one component, `components/shared/SiteLogo.tsx`, which prints no name/motto beside it (without an upload it falls back to the generic mark + site name). Replaced the hand-built logos in the storefront header (now also on mobile), footer, mobile drawer, login, register, forgot/reset/confirm/verify, newsletter manage/unsubscribe, error pages, the four landing-page spots, admin sidebar (full logo expanded and collapsed, 'Admin Panel' kept as a caption), admin mobile bar and admin login; dark surfaces (login panels, admin login, landing page, and any logo in the dark theme) put it on a small white rounded plate. Emails (5 templates) show it on a white plate and drop the name text; the invoice PDF uses the CMS logo when it is a PNG/JPG, else `public/images/brand/invoice-logo.png`. New Admin › Settings › Favicon (`favicon` setting, 512 KB, png/ico/webp/jpg; upload/replace/clear like the loading logo; shared via `settings.favicon` and the LayoutCache allow-list; `app.blade.php` links it, an .ico is never used as the apple-touch icon). Bundled fallbacks regenerated from the new logo: `public/favicon.ico`, `public/apple-touch-icon.png`, `public/images/brand/og-image.jpg` (default social image; `PageSeo::DEFAULT_IMAGE` and its test updated). `public/Shoppydrive.jpg` is now unused and left in place. The dev database's `logo` setting now points at the cropped PNG (`storage/app/public/logos/shoppydrive-logo.png`); production still needs the same upload through the CMS. Loading Logo is unchanged (its own setting). Tests: new `FaviconSettingTest` (6); logo/SEO/mail/invoice/newsletter suites pass. Not verified in a browser.  *Landing-page CMS list and modals (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):* phone rows use the shared `admin-row-card` treatment with the product image, the status pill top-right and the slug on its own full-width row (one line, ellipsis + hover title; desktop unchanged apart from the image); the edit modal's slug and product fields stack on phones; the product field is a searchable `ComboboxField` in both modals; creating or saving a page now redirects to the list (`Admin\LandingPageController::store/update`), which closes the modal instead of opening the editor; the slug prefix label read `/sentinel/` and now reads `/lp/`. `index` now returns a mapped list with `product.image`. New `LandingPageModalFlowTest` (3). Not verified in a browser.  *Bulk-selection bar on phones (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):* the one shared `BulkActionBar` (used by brands, categories, sub-categories, coupons, blogs, flash sales, landing pages, product bundles, popular keywords, SMS patterns) now stacks on phones: count + Clear on the first row, then the actions as equal-width buttons (`grid-flow-col auto-cols-fr`, labels truncate); from `sm` up it is the single row it was. Frontend only; not verified in a browser.  *Landing-page testimonials: text or pictures (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, no migration):* `sections.testimonials` gains `mode` (`text` default | `image`) and `images` (up to 12 paths under `/storage/landing-testimonials/`, validated in `Admin\LandingPageController::sectionRules`). New admin upload `landing-pages.testimonial-images.store` (jpg/png/webp, **capped at 100 KB**, 50 KB suggested; checked in the browser before upload and on the server) through `ImageOptimizer`, with `landing-testimonials` added to `WIDE_DIRECTORIES` so tall phone screenshots keep up to 2400 px on the long side. CMS: a Text reviews / Pictures switch in the testimonial section (both kinds are kept when switching), the picture uploader (multi-select, reorder, remove) and a list of suggested sizes (phone screenshots 720x1600 / 1080x1920; photos 800x800 or 1000x750; 600 px minimum short side). Storefront: `components/landing/TestimonialCarousel`, an auto slider that loops forever (the images are laid out three times and the scroll position is silently moved back to the middle copy once it settles, so autoplay every 3.5 s, arrows, dots and swiping never reach an end; autoplay pauses on hover/touch/focus and for reduced motion; images are never cropped, scaled to one height; a single image is shown static). Tests: `LandingPageTestimonialImagesTest` (5). Not verified in a browser.  *Popular search keywords drag-and-drop reorder (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):* the grip icon on Admin › Popular Search Keywords was decoration only. Rows now reorder the way Admin › Categories does (mouse: native drag of the row; phone/tablet: `useTouchReorder` on the handle, with edge auto-scroll), saved by the new `popular-keywords.reorder` route (`PopularKeywordController::reorder`, position becomes `sort_order` 1..n in one transaction, layout cache flushed, `search_keywords`/update permission). Inline edit refreshes its form values when it opens so a stale sort order can't undo a drag. New `PopularKeywordReorderTest` (3). Not verified in a browser or on a real touch screen.  *Admin customers: Search button and filters (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, no migration):* Admin › Customers gets a Search button (name, email or phone; Enter still works) and three dropdown filters that apply at once: Status (Active = live account that ordered in the last 90 days or joined in the last 30; Inactive = live account with neither; Deleted = anonymized or soft-deleted, which `withTrashed()` now includes), email verified / not verified, and has / has no orders. There is no active flag on `users`, so Active/Inactive are derived (owner chose the 90-day rule; constants `ACTIVE_ORDER_DAYS` / `NEW_ACCOUNT_DAYS` in `CustomerController`). Also fixed: the old search used an ungrouped `orWhere`, which would have escaped any added filter. Deleted customers show a Deleted badge. New `CustomerFiltersTest` (6). Not verified in a browser.  *Live search (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, frontend only):* list search boxes now update their results as you type, 350 ms after typing pauses, through the new `hooks/use-live-search.ts` (Enter and the Search buttons still apply at once; the scroll position is kept). Admin: orders, trashed orders, incomplete orders, products, categories, sub-categories, brands, coupons, flash sales, product bundles, blogs, customers, reviews, returns, received SMS, contact messages and newsletter. Storefront: the products/category listing (a request for older text no longer overwrites what has been typed since), the blog and My Orders. Already live and left alone: the header search suggestions, the brand listing and FAQ filters, and the admin product/customer pickers. Typing counts against the `product-browsing` limiter (default 120/min) on the products page. Not verified in a browser. *Checkout summary, newsletter link, home sliders (2026-11-12, owner requests; FIXED_LOCALLY; frontend only):* the checkout Order Summary card is sticky from `lg` up (its grid column now stretches to the form's height; the card scrolls inside itself if taller than the viewport); the newsletter preview's View Product link works; the home page Shop by category (replacing the animated grid) and Our brands (no scrollbar) now use the new shared `components/shared/AutoSlider.tsx`: scroll-snap row that advances one item every 3 s and wraps to the start, swipeable by touch, mouse-draggable, paused on hover/touch/focus and for reduced motion. No new dependency (no Swiper library). Not verified in a browser. *Profile page (2026-11-12, owner request):* the separate Upload New button is gone (clicking the avatar uploads; Remove stays); the email field is disabled and read-only, is no longer part of the form data, and `ProfileUpdateRequest`/`AccountProfileController::update` no longer accept or act on `email` at all (supersedes the profile-form half of SEC-009; the confirm/cancel routes and `EmailChangeService` are left in place, unreachable from the form). New test in `Account/ProfileUpdateTest`. **12 older tests that drive an email change through the profile form now fail by design** (`EmailChangeConfirmationTest` x8, `Settings/ProfileUpdateTest` x2, `ProfileAreaTest` x1, `EmailVerificationCodeAddressBindingTest` x1) and await owner approval to rewrite or remove. Not verified in a browser. *Account sidebar (2026-11-12, owner request; frontend only):* the nine account pages no longer wrap themselves in `AccountLayout`; `app.tsx` gives them a persistent nested layout `[AppLayout, AccountLayout]` (`ACCOUNT_PAGES`), so switching tabs keeps the sidebar mounted and only the content changes. Not verified in a browser. *Account tabs, no overlay (2026-11-12, owner request):* switching between account pages no longer shows the full-screen loading overlay (`PageLoadingOverlay` skips visits from one `/account*` page to another); `AccountLayout` instead dims only its content area while a different tab loads, so the sidebar and summary card stay untouched. Not verified in a browser. *Account sidebar really fixed (2026-11-12):* the storefront `AppLayout` keyed its animated `<main>` by pathname, which remounted everything inside it (including `AccountLayout`'s sidebar and summary card) on every tab change, so the previous persistent-layout change did not help on its own. `/account*` pages now share one key (`account-area`); `AccountLayout` fades in only the tab content (`motion.div` keyed by path). Not verified in a browser. *Product page bottom gaps (2026-11-12, owner request; spacing only):* `ProductCarousel` (used only on the product detail page) padding 48→16 px, heading gap 32→12 px, smaller heading and arrow buttons; the page's bottom padding 80→24 px; gaps above 'You May Also Like' and between the two carousels reduced. Not verified in a browser. *Navbar scroll (2026-11-12, owner request; `Header.tsx` only):* collapse/shrink animations slowed from 300 to 500 ms with ease-in-out. The 'bar reappears while scrolling down' bug: collapsing the bar changes page height, the browser's scroll-anchoring correction then looks like a scroll up and re-showed it. Scroll handling is now locked for the animation plus 250 ms (reference position re-based throughout), and the bar needs 40 px of real upward travel to come back. Not verified in a browser. *Navbar hairline (2026-11-12):* the header is `sticky -top-px` (1 px overlap) and the top bar paints a 2 px upward shadow in its own colour, hiding the white line some screens showed above it. Not verified on the affected devices. *Admin product form (2026-11-12, owner request; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED, no migration):* Add Product spacing tightened (card/field/column gaps); the red error summary now names each field ('Short description: The short description is required.', 'Variant 1 price: ...') and the client messages are field-specific; short description, description (visible text, so empty editor markup like `<p><br></p>` counts as empty) and at least one image are required, enforced in `ProductRequest` (descriptions on create and update; image required on create, and an update that sends `images` must keep at least one, while one that omits `images` still leaves media alone; friendly `attributes()`/`messages()`). Existing products with an empty description or short description must be filled in on their next save. Tests: 2 new in `ProductNumericLimitsTest`; 76 test payloads across 15 product test files gained the now-required fields; `ProductMediaFileCleanupTest` 'placeholder a product without photos gets' replaced by 'cannot be saved with all photos removed' (old behaviour is gone by design); the 174 product-related tests pass. The edit page only got the required asterisks. The owner's request item 4 was blank, so nothing was done for it. Not verified in a browser. *Remaining:* production length check; confirm in a browser (not done: extension unavailable); CI on push.*Remaining:* production length check; confirm in a browser (not done: extension unavailable); CI on push.

**Custom variant attributes (2026-11-12, owner feature request: variants from any attribute, e.g. Set: 6pcs/12pcs; owner decisions asked first: commit the colour-image work first (done, `6c26e5a`), the attribute image switch *allows* images per value (never blocks saving), several image-enabled attributes allowed per product, the chosen variant shown everywhere using the order snapshot; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; includes a migration):** *Migration* `2026_11_11_213827_generalize_product_variant_attributes`: `attributes.has_images` (Color on; Size on only if an existing image was tied to a size-only variant, so that link survives), `products.variant_attribute_ids` (backfilled [Color, Size] from the old flags), `product_variants.attribute_value_ids` (backfilled from colour/size), `product_media.attribute_value_id` (backfilled from the not-yet-deployed `color_value_id`, or a size-only variant's size) and drops `product_media.color_value_id`; `down()` restores it. No unique index on attribute names (fixtures and possibly old data hold a second "Color"; uniqueness is `AttributeRequest`'s). *Admin:* Attributes page creates/renames/deletes attributes (name unique ignoring case, type select|colour fixed once it has values, "Images per value" switch; delete refused while a product, variant or retired variant uses it); `AttributeValue` delete guard extended to custom-attribute variants. Product form: `ProductVariantManager` rewritten generically (choose and order attributes; one attribute → a variant per value; two or more → combination checklist with Select all; adding an attribute keeps existing rows' data with its first value, removing one merges rows that become identical; bulk-fill unchanged); image tags `value:{id}` for image-enabled attributes. `ProductRequest` accepts `variant_attribute_ids` + `variants.*.value_ids` (exactly one value per attribute, combinations unique) and still translates the old Color/Size payload (attribute taken from the submitted values). `Admin\ProductController::syncVariants()` reconciles by value set, stores values in attribute order and labels via `VariantAttributeLabels`; old colour/size columns written only for products built from Color/Size alone. Bundle admin labels/needs-variant now generic. *Storefront/orders:* product page selectors follow the product's attribute order; gallery/thumbnail logic generic over value images; landing variant names "Name: Value · …"; checkout summary shows the options; customer order detail, admin order view, invoice and the three order emails show the order's snapshot (`OrderItem::variantLabel()`), falling back to the live variant. Renaming an attribute/value rebuilds every variant's labels; order snapshots keep the old names. *Bugs found and fixed during verification:* (1) the old `product_variants_product_color_size_unique` index rejected Blue/M/6pcs + Blue/M/12pcs — caught only in the real admin UI on MySQL; legacy columns now written only for Color/Size-only products, with a regression test; (2) `OrderItem::variantLabel()` read `$this->variant?->attributes` from inside a model, which PHP resolves to the other model's protected `$attributes` array, not the column — now `getAttribute('attributes')`. *Tests:* new `Admin/AttributeManagementTest` (11) and `Admin/CustomVariantAttributeTest` (11); existing colour-image tests moved to `attribute_value_id`; every existing Color/Size test passes unchanged through the payload translation. Full SQLite suite 3062 tests, 2990 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed; Pint, Prettier, tsc clean; ESLint 0 errors (5 pre-existing hook warnings); `npm run build` OK. *MySQL (disposable `shoppydrive_test_customattr`, identity verified, dropped after):* migrated to just before, inserted old-style colour+size, size-only (size-tagged image) and plain products, migrated: flags, value ids, image values and images all as expected, `color_value_id` dropped; rollback restored the exact previous rows. *Browser (headless Chrome on a second local server pointed at that DB):* created an attribute in the new dialog; an existing Color/Size product loaded into the new editor; ticking Set gave every variant 6pcs; added 12pcs, ticked Blue/M/12pcs, bulk-filled, saved; product page showed Color, Size, Set selectors in order, Add to Cart stored the variant and the cart page shows "Set: 12pcs"; landing page names every option. *Known, cosmetic:* MySQL's JSON type reorders object keys, so a label reads e.g. "Set: 12pcs · Size: M · Color: Blue" rather than in the product's order (the old Color/Size labels already showed "Size … Color …"). Adding an attribute to a product gives its variants new ids (old rows deleted, or kept frozen when a cart/order uses them) — the same rule as turning on Color for a Size-only product before. *Status update:* committed `10da616` and pushed with the colour-image commits (owner approved); dev `ShoppyDrive` database migrated for both migrations (owner approved). Originally: the dev `ShoppyDrive` database is migrated for neither this nor the colour-image migration (needs owner approval).

**Attributes: new attributes are text options only (2026-11-12, owner request after spotting the "Shown as: Text options / Colour swatches" choice in the new-attribute dialog — Color already exists, so a second colour-swatch attribute only invited mistakes, e.g. "Material" as swatches would ask every value for a hex code and show blank circles; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; no migration):** removed the "Shown as" select from the attribute dialog; `AttributeRequest` no longer accepts `type`; `AttributeController::store()` always creates `AttributeType::SELECT` and `update()` never touches the type, so the built-in Color keeps its swatches and a stale page that still sends `type` is ignored rather than rejected. Also fixed size-only copy in the value dialog for custom attributes ("Add size / Shown as a size option" → "Add Set value / Shown as an option", placeholder "e.g. 12pcs"). *Tests:* `Admin/AttributeManagementTest` — replaced the two type tests with "a new attribute is always text options, even when colour swatches are asked for" and "editing an attribute never changes its type, so Color keeps its swatches"; 22/22 in the attribute files, 250 tests matching Attribute|Variant|ProductColorImage passed (15 skipped); Pint, Prettier, ESLint, tsc clean; `npm run build` OK.

**Admin › Pixel & CAPI Log (2026-11-12, owner feature request; owner decisions asked first: log the server-side Conversions API events only (browser Pixel fires go GTM → Meta and are invisible to the server — the page says so), the delete button empties *everything* including undelivered events, access through the Permissions matrix; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; no migration):** new `Admin\MetaTrackingLogController` (`admin.pixel-capi-log.index` / `.destroy`, URL `/admin/pixel-capi-log`) over the existing `meta_tracking_events` outbox; new `pixel_capi_log` `AdminSections` entry (group Marketing, abilities `delete`; view implied), sidebar link under Marketing & Tracking. Index: newest first, 25 per page (`AdminListUrl::redirectIfPastLastPage`), no search; per row time, event, status (Success/Failed/Pending/Sending), order/customer, attempts, and an expandable details panel (event id, times, Meta response code, error, page URL, IP, user agent, fbp/fbc, stored event data); status totals; warning when the Conversions API is off/unconfigured; notes the nightly 30-day `data:prune` retention. Destroy: `confirmed` required, `throttle:10,1`, deletes in 1,000-row batches up to the newest id present at the start (events recorded during the clear are kept), writes an `admin` activity-log entry with the count. `SendMetaTrackingEventJob`: `findOrFail` → `find` + return, so a row cleared between the claim and the read stops quietly instead of landing in Failed Jobs (a row cleared before the job runs was already a no-op at the claim). *Tests:* new `Admin/MetaTrackingLogTest` (8): listing/pagination/order/status totals/details, no-section 403, guest → admin login, delete-all incl. pending/sending + activity entry, `confirmed` required, view-only 403 on delete, `canDelete` flag, delivery job for a cleared event neither sends nor fails. `MetaTrackingEventDeliveryTest`, `MetaTrackingEndpointTest`, `AdminRoutePermissionWiringTest`, `AdminPermissionsTest` green. Full SQLite suite 3070 tests: 2995 passed, 72 skipped, 3 failed — all in `ProductionTransportSecurityTest`, caused by the local `.env` `APP_URL` being a LAN IP (TrustHosts rejects `localhost` → 400) plus the running Vite dev server's `public/hot`; that file passes 12/12 with `APP_URL=http://localhost`. Pint clean, ESLint/tsc clean for the touched files, Prettier applied, `npm run build` OK. *Status update (2026-11-12):* committed `63eb1c1` (tracker `47c81c0`, dev Vite config `5c811c8`) and pushed (owner approved); deploy run succeeded and the owner confirmed the page works in production — VERIFIED_IN_PRODUCTION (owner-supplied evidence).

**Checkout emptied while open (2026-11-12, tester report 63: cart emptied while the checkout page stayed open, then Place Order → bare "Oops! An Error Occurred — 422 Unprocessable Content" page; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; no migration):** *Cause:* `Checkout.tsx` only checked for an empty cart on arrival (deliberately — the cart is also emptied right after an order is placed, and that visit must reach the confirmation page), so a cart emptied later from the header cart, another tab or another device (cross-device sync) left an empty checkout; submitting it hit `CheckoutController::placeOrder()`'s `abort_if(..., 422, 'Your cart is empty.')`, which renders Symfony's raw error page. *Fix:* (1) `Checkout.tsx`: the empty-cart effect now watches the item count for the page's whole life: arriving empty still leaves for home silently; emptied after showing items → toast "Your cart is empty, so there is nothing to check out." + `router.visit(home, {replace})`. Skipped while a submit is in flight and once an order is placed (`orderPlacedRef`, set in `onSuccess` only when the response left `checkout/Checkout` — a price-change/cart-issue refusal comes back to the same page and is not an order), so the post-order cart cleanup can never redirect away from confirmation/payment. Uses a had-items ref, not a first-run flag (strict mode runs effects twice). Buy Now unaffected (its item list is never empty). (2) `CheckoutController`: both empty-cart `abort_if(422)` calls → `emptyCartResponse()` = `back()` with `error` flash + `items` validation error ("Your cart is empty. Add a product to your cart before placing an order."); a logged-in customer's reload of /checkout with an empty cart then goes home via `index()`, a guest's via the page effect. *Tests:* new `EmptyCartCheckoutTest` (3: logged-in emptied cart → back to checkout with message, no order, then /checkout → home; guest; submission without an items list). `BuyNowCheckoutTest` 'checkout with no items and no existing cart is rejected' changed from `assertStatus(422)` to redirect + `items` error (intended behaviour change). Full SQLite suite (with `APP_URL=http://localhost`) 3073 tests: 3001 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed. Pint, ESLint, tsc clean for the touched files, Prettier applied, `npm run build` OK. *Browser (headless Chrome against the local dev site, guest):* added a product, opened checkout, removed it on /cart in a second tab → the checkout tab went to home with the toast; visiting /checkout empty → home, no toast; no console errors. Not browser-tested: placing a real order (would create a dev-database order) — covered by the guard described above.

**Wishlist 'Add All to Cart' moved out-of-stock products into the cart (2026-11-12, tester report: suggested removing add-to-cart from the wishlist page; owner chose to keep it and block out-of-stock products instead; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; no migration):** each product card's own Add to Cart already refused out-of-stock products (`ProductCard` toast 'Out of stock'); the gap was `WishlistController::addAllToCart()`, which only skipped hidden/deleted products and products needing a size/colour, never checking stock. *Fix:* variant-less products with `stock_quantity <= 0` are no longer added — they stay on the wishlist, counted in a new `skipped_out_of_stock` JSON field and the message ('1 item is out of stock, so it stayed on your wishlist.' / 'N items are out of stock, so they stayed on your wishlist.'); the flash is `info` (not `success`) whenever anything was skipped. Variant products are unchanged (they were never added — the customer picks a size/colour on the product page). Not changed: adding 1 to a cart line that already holds all remaining stock (same as everywhere else in the cart; checkout's stock check refuses it). *Tests:* 2 new in `WishlistActionsTest` (mixed in-stock + sold-out: only the in-stock one reaches the cart, the sold-out one stays saved, exact JSON + message; all sold out: no cart created, both stay, info message). All 49 wishlist tests green; full SQLite suite (with `APP_URL=http://localhost`) 3075 tests: 3003 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed; Pint clean. No frontend change.

**Storefront toast redesign (2026-11-12, owner request: the website's flash notifications used sonner's default look; redesign for the storefront only, with a premium appear/disappear animation; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED; styling only):** `components/ui/sonner.tsx` renders the same single sonner `Toaster` two ways, chosen by the current URL (`/admin` keeps the old `theme`/`bottom-right` default look, unchanged; every other page gets the new design), following Inertia `navigate` events since the Toaster sits outside the page tree. Storefront: `unstyled` toasts with `sd-toast*` classes, lucide status icons, close button, top-centre (20px; 12px on phones — it no longer sits over the bottom-right chat widget). `resources/css/app.css` (additions only): solid white card, 18px radius, layered soft shadow, status colour (success green / error red / warning amber / info+loading blue) as a tinted icon tile, a soft corner wash and a 3px accent edge — drawn on the card's own background because sonner uses its `::before/::after` for hover/swipe hit areas. Motion: overrides only sonner's `data-mounted='false'` and front `data-removed` states and the timing — enters from above out of an 8px blur and 0.88 scale with a slight spring (700ms), leaves lifting into a blur (420ms, faster); stacking, hover-expand, hover-pause and swipe-to-dismiss stay sonner's own; `prefers-reduced-motion` drops the blur (sonner already drops transitions). No caller changed (`addToast`, `toast.*`, flash toasts all go through it). *Checks:* headless Chrome on the local dev site, desktop 1280px and phone 390px, real toasts raised through the app's own sonner instance: entering (blurred), settled success, collapsed stack of 3, hover-expanded stack, info, leaving (blurred), plain untyped toast; storefront toaster has `sd-toaster`, `/admin/login` still `toaster group`; no console errors. Prettier, tsc clean; `npm run build` OK. No PHP change, no tests needed (styling only).

**CMS-controlled loading logo (2026-11-12, owner request: control the page-loading spinner's logo from the CMS; owner chose "own loader image" only, in Settings ▸ General; committed and pushed 4b77e82, deploy outcome not recorded):** new `loading_logo` Setting (group `general`). `Admin\SettingController`: added to `GENERAL_FIELDS`, validation (`image`, max 2 MB when a file), `getGroup()`, and a new `saveLoadingLogo()` — an upload stores to `public/logos` and deletes the previous file; an explicitly emptied field deletes the file and the row (falls back to the brand logo); any other submitted value (the current URL the form echoes back) is ignored, never stored. Gated by the existing `settings` update permission (not in any `FIELD_GROUPS` sub-section). Exposed through `LayoutCache::PUBLIC_SETTING_KEYS` and `HandleInertiaRequests` shared `settings.loading_logo` (Setting's `FlushesLayoutCache` clears it on save/delete); `AppSettings` type updated. `admin/settings/edit.tsx`: "Loading Logo" `ImageUpload` under Brand Logo. `PageLoadingOverlay.tsx` spins `loading_logo`, else `logo`, else the built-in icon — animation unchanged. *Tests:* new `tests/Feature/Admin/LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` (6: upload + storefront prop, replace deletes old file, echoed/foreign URL ignored, clear removes file and falls back, non-image rejected, no `settings` update permission → 403); with `LayoutCacheTest`, `SettingsSubPagesTest`, `GuestCheckoutTest`, `AdminPermissionsTest`: 56 passed. Pint, ESLint, Prettier, tsc clean on changed files. Dev site confirmed sharing `settings.loading_logo`.

**Loading ring + CMS speed (2026-11-12, owner request: loading logo 4× larger and still; an orange gradient ring (#F58120 → #EE5923) turning on the Y axis around it; speed from the CMS; committed c3ba1bd, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** new shared `components/shared/LoadingLogoSpinner.tsx` (ring = a masked gradient circle, framer-motion `rotateY` 0→360 linear; logo box 66% of the ring = 224px at the default `min(340px, 86vw)`, i.e. 4× the old 56px, sized so a square logo's corners clear the ring; built-in icon fallback kept). `PageLoadingOverlay.tsx` uses it (show/hide timing unchanged) and now preloads the logo, since the loading logo is otherwise first fetched only when the overlay appears. New `loading_spin_seconds` Setting (seconds per turn, 0.3–10, default 1.1 = the old speed): `SettingController` constants, validation, stored as a real number (rounded to 0.1); `LayoutCache::PUBLIC_SETTING_KEYS`; `HandleInertiaRequests` shares it clamped to the bounds; `AppSettings` type. `admin/settings/edit.tsx`: "Loading Ring Speed" slider + number field with a live preview using the same component. *Tests:* `LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` +6 (saved as number + shared, default 1.1, 0.2/10.1/"fast" rejected, stored out-of-range value clamped); with related settings/cache/limits tests 68 passed. Pint, ESLint, Prettier, tsc clean on changed files. *Checks:* headless Chrome, dev site, 1280px and 390px, slowed network: logo 224px (221px on phones) still, ring turning on Y around it, no console errors (dev `/storage` images return 403 locally, so a stand-in image was served for the check). The CMS preview was not opened in a browser.

**Loading ring diameter + logo size from the CMS (2026-11-12, owner request, follows the entry above; committed c3ba1bd, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** new `App\Support\LoadingScreenSettings` holds all three loader numbers (`loading_spin_seconds` 0.3–10 s default 1.1; new `loading_ring_size` 80–600 px default 340; new `loading_logo_size` 32–560 px default 224) — `rules()` for validation, `normalize()` stores real numbers (sizes as whole pixels), `forFrontend()` defaults and clamps for `HandleInertiaRequests`; the speed constants briefly on `SettingController` moved there. Both new keys added to `GENERAL_FIELDS`, `getGroup()`, `LayoutCache::PUBLIC_SETTING_KEYS`, `AppSettings`. `LoadingLogoSpinner.tsx` takes `ringSize`/`logoSize` in px plus `maxSize` (86vw on the storefront): the ring is `min(ring, maxSize)` and the logo scales by the same ratio, so proportions hold on phones. `edit.tsx`: "Loading Screen" field with Speed / Ring diameter / Logo size sliders + exact-value boxes and one live preview (drawn at most 180px, labelled with its scale). *Tests:* `LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` +8 (sizes saved as integers and shared, defaults 340/224, 5 out-of-bounds/non-numeric cases rejected, stored out-of-range clamped to 600/32); with related settings/cache/limits/permissions/review-photo tests 119 passed. Pint, ESLint, Prettier, tsc clean on changed files. Dev-site headless check: shared values arrive, 340px ring/224px logo on desktop, 221px logo at 390px. CMS page itself not opened in a browser.

**Loading Screen field validation + slider overlap (2026-11-12, owner request, follows the entry above; committed c3ba1bd, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** *Overlap:* each value box combined `settingsInputClass` (which has `w-full`) with `w-20` by string concatenation, so `w-full` won and the box covered the slider; now merged with `cn()` (tailwind-merge) to a fixed `w-[4.5rem]`, label `w-24`, slider `min-w-0 flex-1`. *Validation, server* (`LoadingScreenSettings::rules(Request, $stored)` + `attributes()`): a field sent must have a value (`sometimes|required`), be numeric, the speed at most one decimal place (`decimal:0,1`), the sizes whole pixels (`integer`), within the bounds, and the logo size may not exceed the ring diameter — the one submitted with it, else the saved one (default 340). Readable names (speed / ring diameter / logo size) in messages. *Validation, form* (`edit.tsx`): the three values are held as typed text; `loadingValueError()` mirrors the server rules and shows the message under the field as you type (red border, `aria-invalid`/`aria-describedby`); Save is stopped while any is wrong (focuses the first bad field, note beside the button); the preview and slider use the default while a value is invalid. *Tests:* `LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` now also rejects 1.25 s, empty speed, 340.5/224.5 px, empty sizes, logo 201 > ring 200 (exact message), logo 151 > saved ring 150; accepts logo = ring; the earlier 300.4 px case changed to 300. With related settings/cache/limits/permissions/review-photo/shipping/referral tests: 158 passed. Pint, ESLint, Prettier, tsc clean. Class merge verified with tailwind-merge directly; the CMS page itself still not opened in a browser (no admin login used).

**Responsive loader + ring thickness (2026-11-12, owner request: mobile size derived from the desktop settings; thinner ring with a CMS control; committed c3ba1bd, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** the CMS sizes are now the desktop sizes. `LoadingLogoSpinner.tsx` draws the ring at `min(86vw, clamp(0.6·D px, D·100/1024 vw, D px))` — exactly D on screens ≥1024px, shrinking with the screen width, never below 60% of D (fixed `LOADING_PHONE_SCALE`, recommended to and not objected to by the owner; could become a CMS field later); logo and line thickness stay fixed fractions of the drawn diameter. New `responsive` prop (off for the CMS preview, which shows desktop proportions). New `loading_ring_thickness` Setting (1–20 whole px, default 4 — previously a fixed 2.5% of the diameter, ≈8.5px at 340px), added to `LoadingScreenSettings::FIELDS`/`attributes()`, `GENERAL_FIELDS`, `getGroup()`, `LayoutCache::PUBLIC_SETTING_KEYS`, `AppSettings`, the overlay and a fourth CMS slider (same live validation); the field hint explains the desktop/phone sizing. *Tests:* `LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` +5 (thickness 0/21/2.5/empty rejected; saved as integer, default 4, shared, clamped to 20); with related suites 133 passed. Pint, ESLint, Prettier, tsc clean. *Checks:* headless Chrome on the dev site (owner's dev values ring 340 / logo 299 / thickness 4): ring 340/255/204px and logo 299/224/179px at 1280/768/390px widths, thin ring visible, no console errors.

**Newsletter thank-you modal (2026-11-12, tester report: subscribing should get a proper "thank you", in an animated modal matching the design; committed 6eedcca, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** `NewsletterController::subscribe()` now also `Inertia::flash('newsletter', ['status' => subscribed|resubscribed|already_subscribed])` (the old `newsletter_status` session flash is kept; it was never shared to the page, so both forms always said "subscribed"). New `components/shared/NewsletterThankYouModal.tsx` (Radix Dialog + framer-motion, theme colour variables so it matches every storefront theme: card springs up out of a blur, icon pops in with ripple rings, a colour burst for new/returning subscribers, text rises in sequence; per-status icon/title/message echoing the email; "Continue shopping", close button, Escape, outside click; reduced-motion honoured; z-[70]/[71] so it covers the cookie banner (z-65)). New `hooks/use-newsletter-subscribe.ts` shared by the footer form and the Home page form (posts to `route('newsletter.subscribe')`, reads `page.flash.newsletter.status`, opens the modal, clears the field); the footer form now also shows validation/rate-limit errors, which it previously never displayed. *Tests:* `NewsletterTest.php` asserts the flash for all three outcomes, plus a new invalid-email case (error, no flash, no row); newsletter suites 46 passed. ESLint/Prettier/tsc clean (one pre-existing `useMemo` warning in Home.tsx untouched). *Checks:* headless Chrome on the dev site, footer form at 1280px and 390px: "Thank you for subscribing!", then "You're already subscribed" on resubmitting, closes via button and Escape, no console errors. This created one dev-database subscriber, `newsletter-modal-check@example.com`.

**Loading logo load speed (2026-11-12, owner report: sometimes only the ring shows; committed c3ba1bd, pushed cd0d289, deploy outcome not recorded):** cause — the uploaded loading logo was 8252×8190 px (413 KB WebP, ~67 MP), stored as uploaded, so browsers often hadn't downloaded/decoded it when the overlay appeared. Owner will re-upload a 512×512 image (recommended: square, transparent WebP/PNG, under 100 KB). Safeguards: `ImageOptimizer::store()` gains an optional `$maxSide` (existing callers unchanged); the loading logo upload now goes through it at `LoadingScreenSettings::LOGO_STORED_MAX_SIDE` = 1024 (WebP when smaller; the brand `logo` deliberately still isn't, for Outlook); uploads over `LOGO_UPLOAD_MAX_SIDE` = 4096 px per side are rejected with a clear message (GD couldn't resize them in memory and would store them as-is). `PageLoadingOverlay` now keeps the preloaded `Image` in a ref and calls `decode()`, so the decoded logo stays ready for the whole visit; `LoadingLogoSpinner` fades the logo in on load instead of painting it in pieces. CMS hint mentions the limits and auto-resize. *Tests:* `LoadingLogoSettingTest.php` +2 (2000×1500 stored at 1024 longest side; 4097 px rejected with the message); with ImageOptimizer-using, newsletter, cache and settings suites 92 passed. Dev-site headless check: logo visible from the first overlay frame.

**Newsletter welcome email (2026-11-12, owner request after asking whether subscribers get any email with the footer's promised unsubscribe link — they didn't until a campaign was sent; committed and pushed 3bc960c, deploy outcome not recorded):** new `App\Mail\NewsletterWelcomeMail` (queued, `afterCommit`, one-to-one) + `emails/newsletter/welcome.blade.php` (transactional layout: thanks, "Start shopping" button, "Unsubscribe here" with the subscriber's own token link to `newsletter.unsubscribe.confirm`, plus a `List-Unsubscribe` header so mail apps offer their own button; the confirm GET still changes nothing until the POST — FSA-14 unchanged). `NewsletterController::subscribe()` queues it for new and re-activated subscribers only (re-activation carries the rotated token; already-active addresses get nothing, so repeat sign-ups can't flood an inbox); a mail/queue failure is logged without email/token and the subscription still succeeds, same pattern as `manageSubmit()`. Not double opt-in: anyone can still subscribe any address (throttled by `newsletter-signup`), but the owner of that address now hears about it and can unsubscribe in one confirmed click. *Tests:* new `NewsletterWelcomeEmailTest.php` (6: new subscriber gets mail with own token; returning subscriber gets the new token; active subscriber not re-mailed; invalid email not mailed; rendered link + header point at a working confirm page; queue failure still subscribes and logs no email/token); newsletter suites 51 passed. Rendered email checked visually (local dev `/storage` 403 hides the header logo locally only).

**Newsletter unsubscribe pages redesign + reused link goes home (2026-11-12, owner request; committed and pushed 3bc960c, deploy outcome not recorded):** `pages/newsletter/Unsubscribe.tsx` rewritten in the storefront's theme variables (it already sat inside the storefront layout but drew its own logo, grey full-screen background and fixed colours): one animated card per state — *confirm* ("Sorry to see you go", email pill, what they'll miss, "Yes, unsubscribe me" / "Keep me subscribed" stacked on phones, note that order/delivery emails continue), *unsubscribed* (one-time success: "You've been unsubscribed", Continue shopping, how to resubscribe), *invalid* ("This link isn't valid", Get a new link → `newsletter.manage`, Go to home page). Server (`NewsletterController`): `unsubscribe()` sets a one-request session flag `newsletter_unsubscribed` = that email only when its update actually changed a row; `unsubscribeConfirm()` for an inactive subscriber shows the success state only when the flag matches that email, otherwise redirects to `home` with an info toast "You're already unsubscribed from our newsletter." (a reused link or a refresh); the `already_unsubscribed` page state is gone. FSA-14 unchanged: the GET never changes state, invalid/unknown pairs still show the same generic invalid state. Campaign sends already re-check `is_active` at send time (`SendNewsletterChunk`), so unsubscribing is immediate. *Tests:* `NewsletterTest.php` — the confirm test now asserts success page once then home + toast on reuse; new: an old inactive link goes home, a repeated confirm sets no success flag, a flag for one email can't show another as unsubscribed; newsletter suites 37 passed. ESLint/Prettier/tsc clean. *Checks:* headless Chrome on the dev site with a real subscriber (`newsletter-modal-check@example.com`, re-created via the footer form, now unsubscribed): confirm page (1280px, no horizontal overflow) → Yes, unsubscribe → success page → same link again → `/` with the toast shown ~4s; invalid page at 390px. `newsletter/Manage.tsx` keeps its old design (not in scope).

**Unsubscribe blocked by the sign-up rate limit (2026-11-12, owner report: "Yes, unsubscribe me" showed "Too many newsletter requests…"; committed and pushed 3bc960c, deploy outcome not recorded):** the confirm POST (`newsletter.unsubscribe`) shared `throttle:newsletter-signup` (default 5 per 10 min per IP/user) with subscribing, so sign-ups from the same network — on the dev machine, the owner's tests plus this session's headless-browser runs; in production, any shared Bangladeshi mobile-network IP — could stop someone unsubscribing. It needs the subscriber's own 64-char token (nothing to guess), so it now uses `throttle:newsletter-check` (the confirmation page's own limit, default 30/min, still admin-tunable); subscribing keeps the strict limit. `routes/web.php` comment explains why; Admin › Security labels now read "Joining the newsletter, for the same shopper" and "Opening or confirming an unsubscribe link". *Tests:* `SecurityRateLimitsTest` wiring row updated; new `NewsletterTest` case: sign-up limit exhausted (rate_limit error) and a valid-token unsubscribe still succeeds; newsletter + security-limit suites 106 passed. Local route list confirms the new middleware (no route cache).

**Product-promotion newsletter: broken image + 404 View Product (2026-11-12, tester report; FIXED_LOCALLY, NOT COMMITTED):** four defects. (1) The button linked to `config('app.url')/products/{slug}`, but the product page is `product.show` = `/product/{slug}` → 404; now `route('product.show', $product)`, also wrapped around the image. (2) The image `src` was the stored site-relative path (`/storage/…`), which email apps can't resolve; `NewsletterProductMail` now passes absolute `imageUrl`/`productUrl` (`url()` for local paths, http(s) URLs kept). (3) The compose picker listed all media and pre-selected the primary item even when it was a video, and the template's fallback used `media->first()` (often an `.mp4`); `Admin\NewsletterController::compose()` now loads images only, the mail falls back to `Product::primaryImagePath()` (images only), and `send()` rejects a `selected_image` that isn't one of that product's own image paths ("Choose one of this product's images.") — no videos, other products' images or outside addresses. (4) Product photos are WebP (Outlook desktop can't show WebP): `send()` makes one email-safe copy per send via new `ImageOptimizer::emailJpeg()` (JPEG, transparency filled white, longest side 1200 for the 600px email on 2x screens) at `public/newsletter-images/…jpg`, shared by every chunk and kept (emails are opened later; not touched by `newsletter:prune-attachments`, which only scans the local disk); falls back to the original if the copy can't be made. `SendNewsletterChunk` eager-loads `images` instead of `media`. *Tests:* new `tests/Feature/Admin/NewsletterProductPromotionTest.php` (10: link is `/product/{slug}` and opens 200; image src absolute; primary image used, never a video; no-image product still sends; compose lists images only; chosen WebP 2400px → queued JPEG ≤1200; no choice → copy of primary image; video / other product's image / outside URL rejected, nothing batched); with newsletter send/pacing/prune/manage and ImageOptimizer suites 51 passed. Not visually rendered (dev `/storage` 403).

**Rejected form submissions jumped to the top of the page (2026-11-12, tester report: every backend error — field validation, out of stock — sent the user to the top instead of to the message; FIXED_LOCALLY, committed, NOT PUSHED):** confirmed real and site-wide: Inertia's `preserveScroll` defaults to `false`, so a POST the server sends `back()` with errors renders as a fresh page scrolled to the top. Fix in new `resources/js/lib/form-error-scroll.ts`, wired once in `app.tsx`: (1) `createInertiaApp({ defaults: { visitOptions: preserveScrollOnErrors } })` — every non-GET visit that doesn't set `preserveScroll` itself gets Inertia's built-in `'errors'` mode (keep position only when the response has errors; a successful submission landing on a new page still starts at the top; explicit choices untouched). (2) `registerFormErrorScroll()` — after a rejected submission (Inertia `error` event, or a `success` whose page carries a `flash.error` banner; background `async`/partial `only` reloads skipped), two frames later it smoothly centres the first visible error: `[aria-invalid="true"]` (the shared ui form controls), the field each error key names (`name` incl. dotted→bracket and `[]`, or `id`), or `[data-form-error]`; toasts are never targets; with no on-page error (toast only) the page just stays put; text fields get focus on fine-pointer devices only (no phone keyboard over the message); reduced-motion uses an instant jump. `data-form-error` added to `InputError`, `AlertError`, the `flash.error` banners on Checkout (where `InsufficientStockException`'s `items` error has no field), MobilePayment, account Orders/OrderDetail, admin failed-jobs/audit-trail/pixel-capi-log, and the admin product create/edit error summaries (whose own scroll-to-summary effect now targets the same element). No PHP change. *Checks:* ESLint/Prettier/tsc clean on all changed files; headless Chrome on the dev site, Contact page scrolled to the bottom, real form submitted empty (native validation bypassed; server rejects, nothing saved): 1280px scrollY 769 → 179 with the first error 404px down an 800px viewport; 390px 2189 → 730, first error at 390px — previously both would have gone to 0. Checkout's stock-error banner path not exercised in a browser (needs a real stock shortage).

**Empty-cart "Start shopping" stayed on the same page (2026-11-12, owner report; FIXED_LOCALLY, committed, NOT PUSHED):** the empty Cart *page* button goes to `products.index`, but the empty cart *drawer*'s button (`CartDrawer.tsx`, via `EmptyState`) linked to `/` and never closed the drawer — clicked on the home page nothing visibly happened, and elsewhere the open drawer covered the page it navigated to. The drawer button now goes to `route('products.index')` (same as the Cart page) and closes the drawer. `EmptyState`'s link now also runs `action.onClick` when given (no existing caller passes both, so nothing else changes). *Checks:* tsc/Prettier clean (the `ui` folder is ESLint-ignored); headless Chrome on the dev site at 1280px and 390px: home page, empty cart, open drawer, Start shopping → `/products`, drawer closed, no console errors.

**Coupon field removed from /cart (2026-11-12, tester report: a coupon applied on /cart only reduced the price there and was gone at checkout; committed and pushed fead24e):** root cause — a /cart coupon lived only in the browser's cart store; for a logged-in customer every Inertia page visit re-hydrates the store from the server cart (`app.tsx` `hydrateCart(currentCart)`), which carries no coupon, so it was wiped on the way to /checkout before Checkout's BIZ-003 seed (`useState(() => storeCoupon)`) could read it (guests kept it). Per the tester's recommendation the field is removed rather than changing the delicate cross-device sync: `pages/cart/Cart.tsx` drops the promo code form/active-coupon chip, the coupon-validate fetch and the Discount row; the Estimated Total is now subtotal + shipping estimate + tax (never a coupon discount checkout might not honour), and a note reads "Have a promo code? You can apply it at checkout." Checkout's own coupon field and its store sync are unchanged; `coupon.validate` is still used by checkout. *Checks:* ESLint/Prettier/tsc clean; headless Chrome on the dev site: product added, /cart summary shows the note, no code input, Estimated Total = Subtotal (৳2,000.55), no console errors.

**Admin › Error Log (2026-11-12, owner request: view laravel.log in a structured way, no database; owner chose the built-in page with a Clear button; committed and pushed fead24e):** new `App\Support\LaravelLogReader` (file-based, read-only apart from clear): lists only files matching `laravel.log` / `laravel-YYYY-MM-DD.log` in storage/logs (production always uses the `daily` channel, 30 days), newest first; a request can only *name* one of those, never a path. Reads only the last `WINDOW_BYTES` (4 MB) of a file, splits it at each `[date] env.LEVEL: ` record start (a cut-off first record is skipped), extracts message (without the appended JSON context), level, environment, exception class + location (from the context's `[object] (Class(code: …): … at path:line)`, JSON-unescaped, path made relative) and the full text capped at 20 KB per entry; `clear()` empties the file (LOCK_EX) so logging carries on. New `Admin\ErrorLogController` (reader injected via the container): `index` (file + level filters validated against the listed files / Monolog levels, level counts, 25 per page, past-last-page redirect), `download`, `clear` (requires `confirmed`, activity-log entry "Error log {file} cleared."). Routes `admin.error-log.{index,download,clear}` in an `admin.mother` group (download/clear also `throttle:mother-actions`), documented in `AdminRoutePermissionWiringTest`'s exempt list like Database Backup; sidebar "Error Log" (ScrollText, `motherOnly`) under Database Backup. Page `admin/error-log/index.tsx`: file picker (when >1), Download, Clear Log (ConfirmDialog warning it empties the whole file), level filter chips with counts, table (time + environment, level badge, message + exception · location), expandable full text/stack trace, pagination, notice when only the end of a large file is shown. Ziggy regenerated locally. Dev check: real 15 MB laravel.log → last 4 MB = 10,425 entries parsed in 19 ms. *Tests:* new `tests/Feature/Admin/ErrorLogTest.php` (12, each in its own temp folder: entries newest first with exception/location/stack; level filter; daily files listed newest first and selectable, other files ignored; large file → only the end, no partial entry; browser.log / `../../.env` / missing file refused for view, download and clear; download; clear empties but keeps the file and is audited; confirmation required; empty folder; non-mother admins 403 on all three). Full suite: 3,123 passed, 72 skipped, 0 failed; Pint clean; ESLint/Prettier/tsc clean on the new page and layout. The page itself not opened in a browser (no admin login used).

**Unused uploaded files: clean-up on replace/delete + `storage:audit` (2026-11-12, owner request after a read-only analysis — steps 1 and 3 of its recommendations; committed and pushed fead24e):** analysis found only avatars, review photos, the site/loading logo, newsletter attachments and restore uploads were cleaned up; categories, sub-categories, brands, blog featured images, hero slides, promotional banners, product bundles, landing-page slides, text-editor images and product media all left replaced/deleted files on disk (dev: 1,740 of 1,795 public files, 100 MB, referenced by nothing — inflated by dev resets, but the mechanisms are real).
